   342	      function triggerSecondaryRecovery({
   343	        recoveryDecision,
   344	        effectiveSecondaryLanguage,
   345	        secondaryActiveCues,
   346	        primaryActiveCues,
   347	        secondaryCueText,
   348	        primaryCueText,
   349	        currentPrimaryText,
   350	        hasFreshCurrentPrimary,
   351	        mergedSubtitleHealth,
   352	      }) {
   353	        if (
   354	          recoveryDecision.action !== "recover" &&
   355	          recoveryDecision.action !== "force-rebind"
   356	        ) {
   357	          return;
   358	        }
   359	
   360	        const secondaryTrackFoundBefore = Boolean(state.secondaryTrack);
   361	        const secondaryActiveCuesLengthBefore =
   362	          getTrackActiveCuesLength?.(state.secondaryTrack) ?? 0;
   363	
   364	        runSecondaryResolverProbeIfNeeded({
   365	          effectiveSecondaryLanguage,
   366	          secondaryCueText,
   367	        });
   368	
   369	        logContent?.("secondary recovery trigger started", {
   370	          effectiveSecondaryLanguage,
   371	          secondaryTrackFoundBefore,
   372	          secondaryActiveCuesLengthBefore,
   373	          renderInvoked: false,
   374	        });
   375	
   376	        logContent?.(
   377	          "secondary recovery trigger",
   378	          buildSecondarySyncLogPayload({
   379	            effectiveSecondaryLanguage,
   380	            secondaryActiveCues,
   381	            primaryActiveCues,
   382	            secondaryCueText,
   383	            primaryCueText,
   384	            currentPrimaryText,
   385	            hasFreshCurrentPrimary,
   386	            mergedSubtitleHealth,
   387	            extra: {
   388	              reason: "sync_interval",
   389	              missCount: recoveryDecision.secondaryLane.missCount,
   390	              forceRebind: recoveryDecision.action === "force-rebind",
   391	              terminated: recoveryDecision.secondaryLane.terminated,
   392	              missLimitReached: recoveryDecision.action === "terminated",
   393	            },
   394	          }),
   395	        );
   396	
   397	        syncSecondarySubtitleTrack?.({
   398	          reason: recoveryDecision.reason,
   399	          forceRebind: recoveryDecision.action === "force-rebind",
   400	        });
   401	
   402	        const secondaryTrackFoundAfter = Boolean(state.secondaryTrack);
   403	        const secondaryActiveCuesLengthAfter =
   404	          getTrackActiveCuesLength?.(state.secondaryTrack) ?? 0;
   405	
   406	        logContent?.("secondary recovery trigger finished", {
   407	          effectiveSecondaryLanguage,
   408	          secondaryTrackFoundBefore,
   409	          secondaryTrackFoundAfter,
   410	          secondaryActiveCuesLengthBefore,
   411	          secondaryActiveCuesLengthAfter,
   412	          renderInvoked: true,
   413	        });
   414	      }

  3346	  function hasRecoverableInitialCue() {
  3347	    const currentTime = state.video?.currentTime ?? 0;
  3348	    const tracks = [
  3349	      state.primaryTrack,
  3350	      state.secondaryTrack,
  3351	      secondaryTrackBound,
  3352	    ];
  3353	
  3354	    for (let i = 0; i < tracks.length; i++) {
  3355	      const track = tracks[i];
  3356	      if (!canReadCueFromTrack(track)) continue;
  3357	      if (getTrackActiveCuesLength(track) > 0) return true;
  3358	      if (getCurrentCueText(track, currentTime)) return true;
  3359	    }
  3360	
  3361	    return false;
  3362	  }

   425	    // secondary track の再解決と再同期を行い、必要なら nearby rebuild まで進める。
   426	    function syncSecondarySubtitleTrack(
   427	      video,
   428	      requestedLang,
   429	      renderSecondarySubtitleOverride,
   430	      options = {},
   431	    ) {
   432	      if (!video) return;
   433	
   434	      // 表示更新を抑止するかどうかを受け取る。
   435	      const suppressRender = options.suppressRender === true;
   436	
   437	      // bind 済み track をいったん外して再取得するかを受け取る。
   438	      const forceRebind = options.forceRebind === true;
   439	
   440	      const previousBoundTrack = secondaryTrackBound;
   441	
   442	      if (DEBUG_SECONDARY_SUBS) {
   443	        logContent(
   444	          "secondary sync",
   445	          getSecondaryTrackDebugPayload(requestedLang, secondaryTrackBound),
   446	        );
   447	      }
   448	
   449	      if (forceRebind) {
   450	        unbindSecondarySubtitleTrack();
   451	      }
   452	
   453	      const track = resolveSecondarySubtitleTrack(video, requestedLang);
   454	      const sameTrackRef = Boolean(track && previousBoundTrack === track);
   455	      const resolvedTrackActiveCuesLength = (() => {
   456	        try {
   457	          return track?.activeCues?.length ?? 0;
   458	        } catch (_) {
   459	          return -1;
   460	        }
   461	      })();
   462	      const resolvedTrackCuesLength = (() => {
   463	        try {
   464	          return track?.cues?.length ?? 0;
   465	        } catch (_) {
   466	          return -1;
   467	        }
   468	      })();
   469	
   470	      logContent("secondary-sync resolver-selected", {
   471	        reason: "syncSecondarySubtitleTrack",
   472	        requestedLang: requestedLang || "",
   473	        forceRebind,
   474	        suppressRender,
   475	        currentTime: getCurrentTime(),
   476	        boundTrackExistsBefore: Boolean(previousBoundTrack),
   477	        sameTrackRef,
   478	        previousBoundTrackLanguage: previousBoundTrack?.language || "",
   479	        previousBoundTrackMode: previousBoundTrack?.mode || "",
   480	        selectedTrackExists: Boolean(track),
   481	        ...getSecondaryTrackObservation(track, "selectedTrack"),
   482	      });
   483	
   484	      logContent("secondary sync raw", {
   485	        requestedLang: requestedLang || "",
   486	        suppressRender,
   487	        forceRebind,
   488	        boundTrackExistsBefore: Boolean(previousBoundTrack),
   489	        resolvedTrackExists: Boolean(track),
   490	        sameTrackRef,
   491	        boundTrackLanguageBefore: previousBoundTrack?.language || "",
   492	        boundTrackModeBefore: previousBoundTrack?.mode || "",
   493	        resolvedTrackLanguage: track?.language || "",
   494	        resolvedTrackKind: track?.kind || "",
   495	        resolvedTrackMode: track?.mode || "",
   496	        resolvedTrackCuesLength,
   497	        resolvedTrackActiveCuesLength,
   498	        currentTime: getCurrentTime(),
   499	      });
   500	
   501	      if (!track) {
   502	        unbindSecondarySubtitleTrack();
   503	        if (!suppressRender) {
   504	          (renderSecondarySubtitleOverride || renderSecondarySubtitle)(
   505	            "",
   506	            null,
   507	          );
   508	        }
   509	        return;
   510	      }
   511	
   512	      const shouldRebindBecauseUnreadable =
   513	        sameTrackRef &&
   514	        resolvedTrackCuesLength > 0 &&
   515	        resolvedTrackActiveCuesLength === 0;
   516	
   517	      if (shouldRebindBecauseUnreadable) {
   518	        logContent("secondary-sync rebind-required", {
   519	          reason: "sameTrackButUnreadableAtCurrentTime",
   520	          requestedLang: requestedLang || "",
   521	          currentTime: getCurrentTime(),
   522	          sameTrackRef,
   523	          forceRebind,
   524	          resolvedTrackLanguage: track?.language || "",
   525	          resolvedTrackMode: track?.mode || "",
   526	          resolvedTrackCuesLength,
   527	          resolvedTrackActiveCuesLength,
   528	        });
   529	      }
   530	
   531	      if (secondaryTrackBound !== track || forceRebind || shouldRebindBecauseUnreadable) {
   532	        bindSecondarySubtitleTrack(track);
   533	        rebuildCurrentSceneSubtitleBlocks();
   534	        return;
   535	      }
   536	
   537	      if (!suppressRender) {
   538	        (renderSecondarySubtitleOverride || renderSecondarySubtitle)(
   539	          getCurrentCueText(track),
   540	          track,
   541	        );
   542	      }
   543	
   544	      rebuildCurrentSceneSubtitleBlocks();
   545	    }
   546	
   547	    // runtime / current cue / sequence を 1 つにまとめ、
   548	    // controller が recovery 判定に使う merged subtitle health を組み立てる。
   549	    // truth source は SubtitleBlockSequence 側の sequenceHealth で、runtime は補助観測として扱う。
   550	    function buildMergedSubtitleHealth({
   740	        state.nearbyRebuildHoldView = holdView;
   741	        armNearbyRebuildGuard(holdBlock);
   742	
   743	        logContent("current subtitle view hold updated", {
   744	          reason: "nearbyRebuildHold",
   745	          source: "nearbyRebuild",
   746	          sourceReason: holdBlock?.sourceReason ?? null,
   747	          guardActive: Boolean(
   748	            nearbyRebuildGuard?.consumeOnNextPrimaryCueChange,
   749	          ),
   750	          withinSeekWindow: isWithinNearbyRebuildSeekWindow(),
   751	          startTime: holdBlock?.startTime ?? null,
   752	          endTime: holdBlock?.endTime ?? null,
   753	          hasPrimarySignal: Boolean(holdBlock?.hasPrimarySignal),
   754	          hasSecondarySignal: Boolean(holdBlock?.hasSecondarySignal),
   755	        });
   756	
   757	        // truth 台帳は触らず、current shared UI と overlay だけを短時間支える。
   758	        state.currentSubtitleView = holdView;
   759	        updateOverlayFromView(holdView);
   760	
   761	        if (secondaryTrack) {
   762	          renderSecondarySubtitle(holdBlock.secondaryText || "", secondaryTrack);
   763	        }
   764	
   765	        renderPanel();
   766	
   767	        return;
   768	      }
   769	
   770	      // primary cue 一覧から現在時刻に最も近い cue index を求める。
   771	      let closestIndex = 0;
   772	      let closestDelta = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
   773	      for (let i = 0; i < allPrimaryCues.length; i++) {
   774	        const cue = allPrimaryCues[i];
   775	        const start = Number(cue?.startTime ?? 0);
   776	        const end = Number(cue?.endTime ?? 0);
   777	        const center = (start + end) / 2;
   778	        const delta = Math.abs(center - currentTime);
   779	        if (delta < closestDelta) {
   780	          closestDelta = delta;
   781	          closestIndex = i;
   782	        }
   783	      }
   784	
   785	      // current 近傍だけを切り出した primary window を nearby rebuild 入力として使う。
   786	      const windowStart = Math.max(0, closestIndex - 1);
   787	      const windowEnd = Math.min(allPrimaryCues.length, closestIndex + 2);
   788	      const windowPrimaryCues = allPrimaryCues.slice(windowStart, windowEnd);
   789	
   790	      // secondary は full cues を使い、pairing 側に探索余地を残す。
   791	      const windowSecondaryCues = allSecondaryCues;
   792	
   793	      // subtitle blocks API の有無を nearby rebuild 前に確認する。
   794	      const blockApi = window.ATVB?.subtitleBlocks || {};
   795	      const hasBuildSubtitleBlockSequence =
   796	        typeof blockApi.buildSubtitleBlockSequence === "function";
   797	
   798	      if (!hasBuildSubtitleBlockSequence) {
   799	        logContent("nearby rebuild skipped", {
   800	          reason: "subtitle_blocks_api_missing",
   801	          primaryTrackFound: Boolean(primaryTrack),
   802	          primaryCuesLength: allPrimaryCues.length,
   803	          secondaryTrackFound: Boolean(secondaryTrack),
   804	          secondaryCuesLength: Array.isArray(allSecondaryCues)
   805	            ? allSecondaryCues.length
   806	            : -1,
   807	        });
   808	        return;
   809	      }
   810	
   811	      // nearby rebuild 用の入力から SubtitleBlockSequence を再構成する。
   812	      const blockResult = blockApi.buildSubtitleBlockSequence({
   813	        primaryCues: windowPrimaryCues,
   814	        secondaryCues: windowSecondaryCues,
   815	        now: currentTime,
   816	        previousBlocks: getPreviousSubtitleBlocks(),
   817	        cleanCueText,
   818	        rebuildReason: "rebuildCurrentScene",
   819	      });
   820	
   821	      setSubtitleBlocks(blockResult, "rebuildCurrentScene");
   822	
   823	      const sequenceHealth = blockResult?.meta?.sequenceHealth || null;
   824	
   825	      const mergedSubtitleHealth = buildMergedSubtitleHealth({
   826	        primaryTrack,
   827	        secondaryTrack,
   828	        pCue,
   829	        pText,
   830	        sCue,
   831	        sText,
   832	        sequenceHealth,
   833	      });
   834	
   835	      lastMergedSubtitleHealth = mergedSubtitleHealth;
   836	
   837	      // sequence current が取れない場合でも最低限の fallback block を作る。
   838	      const currentBlock = getCurrentSubtitleBlockFromSequence(blockResult) || {
   839	        startTime: pCue?.startTime ?? null,
   840	        endTime: pCue?.endTime ?? null,
   841	        primaryText: pText || "",
   842	        secondaryText: sText || "",
   843	        hasPrimarySignal: Boolean(pText),
   844	        hasSecondarySignal: Boolean(sText),
   845	        sourceReason: "nearbyRebuild:fallback",
   846	        updatedAt: Date.now(),
   847	      };
   848	
   849	      currentBlock.sourceReason = "nearbyRebuild";
   850	
   851	      setCurrentSubtitleBlock(currentBlock, "nearbyRebuild");
   852	      armNearbyRebuildGuard(currentBlock);
   853	
   854	      logContent("current subtitle block updated", {
   855	        reason: "nearbyRebuild",
   856	        source: "nearbyRebuild",
   857	        sourceReason: currentBlock?.sourceReason ?? null,
   858	        guardActive: Boolean(nearbyRebuildGuard?.consumeOnNextPrimaryCueChange),
   859	        withinSeekWindow: isWithinNearbyRebuildSeekWindow(),
   860	        startTime: currentBlock?.startTime ?? null,
   861	        endTime: currentBlock?.endTime ?? null,
   862	        hasPrimarySignal: Boolean(currentBlock?.hasPrimarySignal),
   863	        hasSecondarySignal: Boolean(currentBlock?.hasSecondarySignal),
   864	      });
   865	
   866	      // panel / overlay 共通 view を sequence から解決する。
   867	      const overlaySequence = getSubtitleBlockSequence();
   868	      const subtitleViewResolver = root.subtitleViewResolver || null;
   869	      const subtitleView =
   870	        subtitleViewResolver &&
   871	        typeof subtitleViewResolver.resolveUiSubtitleView === "function"
   872	          ? subtitleViewResolver.resolveUiSubtitleView(
   873	              overlaySequence?.blocks,
   874	              overlaySequence?.currentIndex,
   875	              overlaySequence?.meta,
   876	            )
   877	          : null;
   878	
   879	      state.currentSubtitleView = subtitleView;
   880	
   881	      if (subtitleView) {
   882	        updateOverlayFromView(subtitleView);
   883	      } else {
   884	        updateOverlayFromBlock(currentBlock);
   885	      }
   886	
   887	      if (secondaryTrack) {
   888	        renderSecondarySubtitle(sText, secondaryTrack);
   889	      }
   890	
   891	      renderPanel();
   892	    }
   893	
   894	    // primary cue change を基準に full rebuild を行い、必要なら 1 回だけ nearby current / hold view を優先する。
   895	    function onPrimaryCueChange() {
   896	      logContent("cue-controller onPrimaryCueChange entered", {
   897	        hasATVB: !!window.ATVB,
   898	      });
   899	
   900	      const currentTime = getCurrentTime();
   901	      const primaryTrack = getPrimaryTrack();
   902	      const secondaryTrack = getSecondaryTrack();
   903	      const pCue = getCurrentCue(primaryTrack, currentTime);
   904	      const pText = cleanCueText(pCue);
   905	      const sCue = getCurrentCue(secondaryTrack, currentTime);
   906	      const sText = cleanCueText(sCue);
   907	
   908	      if (DEBUG_PANEL_PROBE) {
   909	        logContent("cuechange track probe", {
   910	          primaryTrackLanguage: primaryTrack?.language,
  1040	          ),
  1041	        });
  1042	        consumeNearbyRebuildGuard();
  1043	      } else {
  1044	        clearNearbyRebuildGuard();
  1045	        setCurrentSubtitleBlock(currentBlock, "onPrimaryCueChange");
  1046	        logContent("current subtitle block updated", {
  1047	          reason: "onPrimaryCueChange",
  1048	          source: "primaryCueChange",
  1049	          sourceReason: currentBlock?.sourceReason ?? null,
  1050	          guardActive: Boolean(
  1051	            nearbyRebuildGuard?.consumeOnNextPrimaryCueChange,
  1052	          ),
  1053	          withinSeekWindow: isWithinNearbyRebuildSeekWindow(),
  1054	          startTime: currentBlock?.startTime ?? null,
  1055	          endTime: currentBlock?.endTime ?? null,
  1056	          hasPrimarySignal: Boolean(currentBlock?.hasPrimarySignal),
  1057	          hasSecondarySignal: Boolean(currentBlock?.hasSecondarySignal),
  1058	        });
  1059	      }
  1060	
  1061	      if (shouldUseHoldView) {
  1062	        state.currentSubtitleView = holdView;
  1063	        updateOverlayFromView(holdView);
  1064	      } else if (subtitleView) {
  1065	        state.currentSubtitleView = subtitleView;
  1066	        updateOverlayFromView(subtitleView);
  1067	      } else {
  1068	        updateOverlayFromBlock(currentBlock);
  1069	      }
  1070	
  1071	      if (secondaryTrack) {
  1072	        renderSecondarySubtitle(sText, secondaryTrack);
  1073	      }
  1074	
  1075	      renderPanel();
  1076	    }
  1077	
  1078	    return {
  1079	      getBoundSecondaryTrack,
  1080	      unbindSecondarySubtitleTrack,
  1081	      bindSecondarySubtitleTrack,
  1082	      syncSecondarySubtitleTrack,
  1083	      onCueChange,
  1084	      onPrimaryCueChange,
  1085	      getMergedSubtitleHealth: () => lastMergedSubtitleHealth,
  1086	      getLaneStates: () => laneStates,
  1087	      evaluateSecondaryRecovery,
  1088	    };
  1089	  }
